# Exception Report — Eastsound WWTP Upgrade Phase I

Merge of the five lane outputs (Civil & Site, Process & Mechanical, Electrical & Controls, Structural & Building, Contract & General). Public bid set; test bed only.

- **Version:** Merge rev2, 2026-09-29 (rev0 + Civil & Site resplit pass + updated Electrical & Controls and Contract & General Issues files) · **Owner:** Merge thread · **Built from:** the 15 lane files, 00–04 and Ledger_Schema.csv. No library document was opened; the one precedence question (drawing vs spec) was answered from the Contract & General note for 00 73 00.
- **Columns:** # · Type · Item · Source A · Source B · Which governs (only where a rule decides it: Add. 4, or the 00 73 00 SC 2 order of precedence) · Disposition (blank — owner enters real conflict / lane error / acceptable; drop-down in the .xlsx).
- **Rules:** Add. 4 governs over base documents. Nothing is averaged or resolved silently: where no rule decides, the row says so. Row references use the lane Ledger file and data-row number, e.g. "Civil_and_Site_Ledger.csv row 73"; the same row carries a "[Merge] Exceptions: #n" pointer in Project_Ledger.
- **Ledger:** 455 lane rows → 447 Project Ledger rows (7 combined rows replace 15 lane rows).

|---|---|
| Same tag, different attributes | 17 |

## Merge rules and judgment calls

- **Combine rule applied as written:** same Tag, or same PROPOSED name in the same Area/Building. Matching ignores case, spaces, hyphens and # (assumption: "DO #1" = "DO-1"; "PROPOSED-BLOWER-BUILDING" = "PROPOSED-Blower-Building").
- **Collision guard:** SD-1 is two different items (storm drain alignment vs sludge pump), so it is not combined.
- **Added type — "Same item, different tags (not combined)":** item groups the rule cannot combine because the lanes used different tags or Area/Building wording. Listed so the Ledger's double counts are visible; the Ledger itself follows the rule.
- **Coverage check ("Drawing with no spec, or spec with no drawing")** runs at item level: a row counts as covered when any row for the same item (same-item groups, same-name pairs, Contract & General roll-ups) cites a sheet or a technical section (Divisions 02–46, technical appendices). Rows that are only Demolished, Existing or Temporary are left out.
- **Drawing vs spec:** where a conflict is drawing vs technical specification, "Which governs" cites 00 73 00 SC 2 (main spec p.88), as recorded in the Contract & General note for 00 73 00 (Verified): precedence runs Change Orders, Contract Form, Addenda, permits, Drawings, Supplemental Conditions, Technical Specifications, District standards, WSDOT drawings, WSDOT specs — Drawings rank above Technical Specifications. Drawing vs drawing and spec vs spec stay Unresolved.
- **PROPOSED tags:** kept exactly as the lanes wrote them; every row carrying one is listed for approval, with any printed tag or competing tag found for the same item.

## 2. Same item, different tags (not combined) (77)

| # | Type | Item | Source A | Source B | Which governs | Disposition |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|

## Merge workflow log

- **Merge run 1 (failure):** tool budget ran out after the Wiki; Ledger and Exception Report not written. Same failure mode as Setup run 1. **Fix applied in run 2:** item groupings decided once, then one build script writes all four files before any further reading.
- **Test case — combine rule under-combines (logged, not changed):** 11 same-name PROPOSED pairs differ only in Area/Building wording or "Not stated", and 77 same-item groups carry different tags across lanes, so the Ledger double counts. **Proposed fix for the lane prompts:** a controlled Area/Building list; "Not stated" matches any area; printed tags before PROPOSED; one tag convention for PROPOSED names; a Merge crosswalk step (same-item groups) before combining.
- **Test case — tag collision:** SD-1 names two different items; "PROPOSED-Dewatering system" names two different items. **Fix:** the combine rule needs a type check (Name or Discipline overlap) before combining on tag alone.
- **Test case — lane Issue numbers are ambiguous after merging:** "Issue 18" means different things in two lanes. **Fix applied:** the Project Wiki names the lane and Issues file under every note; **proposed:** lane-prefixed Issue IDs in the lane prompts.
- **Retrieval test — passed:** the drawing-vs-spec precedence needed by four lanes was answered from the Contract & General Wiki note (00 73 00, SC 2) without opening the library.
- **Design gaps logged by the lanes (for the Setup and Composer threads, not exceptions):** Read Method misses graphics-only legends and label-only text layers (Civil & Site CS-05, CS-11; Process & Mechanical log 9; Electrical & Controls log: image-only tables and PLC I/O pairings); Submittal / Testing columns miss drawing-note and reference-document requirements (Civil & Site CS-37); the weakest-tag rule pulls most rows to Inferred through the Bid Item mapping (Process & Mechanical log 3; Contract & General log); duplicate spec bodies (Contract & General Issues 1.13, 1.18); concurrent-session writes need a lock (Contract & General log).
